# Unified exhaustive claim audit

Baseline `40b9b0708d888a015abe5043bb273c3c6ee601ae`. Manuscript SHA256 `5ee7257be5a09c9862c2ead2f98416e1e8d1b2fa875624ca009df5cb95934bbb`. Read-only audit; scratch outputs only. Exact inputs and commands: `input_manifest.json`, `commands_run.md`, `coverage_integrity_report.json`. This is an AI evidence audit, not human peer review or publication certification.

## Reading the ledger

1041 audit units = 874 exact manuscript inventory units + 44 P2 supplements + 113 P3 supplements + 10 generated-caption/note supplements. These are **not 1,041 independent scientific findings**. Counts include headings, references, table cells, whole-row conjunctions and semantic subclaims. Dispositions: {'needs qualification': 112, 'survives': 925, 'must be cut': 4}. Status ‘survives’ means only the bounded statement survives its cited evidence check, not that it belongs in the main Path B paper or that any source label is experimentally valid. The CSV preserves all upstream types, including `projection`. Four whole-row cuts remain PENDING; other rows require partial cuts/qualifications.

Supplement Claim text is the exact parent/context, or AST-assembled caption text explicitly identified in Coverage; Coverage names the assessed subclaim and Parents lists overlapping inventory IDs. Repeated parent/context quotations are intentional and are not counted as extra independent observations. Different parent/subclaim statuses are not automatically disagreements. `reconciliation_log.csv` records explicit changes. Source hashes and upstream original status are preserved in CSV. No manuscript wording has been changed.

Evidence paths inside upstream prose are original locators, not claims that those paths exist in this VM. Use `input_manifest.json` and `path_resolution.md`; raw copyrighted sources are excluded from this package.

| P2-S009 | Splitting by connected components of the Morgan-Tanimoto >=0.70 graph gives AUC 1.000 for descriptor logistic regression, fingerprint logistic regression, RBF-SVM, random forest and nearest-active similarity, and 0.982 for equal fusion. This grouping is not nested within exact-scaffold grouping: four of five component folds share Murcko scaffolds across training/test while enforcing the <0.70 cross-boundary similarity rule. Different folds and training distributions can explain different pooled AUCs. High scores persist under this particular neighbourhood separation, but do not isolate a causal learning mechanism.
